3. Main optimization on n_parent using MD:
   function `md_optimize(a0, time_limit, beta_start, beta_end, max_iter, seed)`:
   - a=norm_sum1(a0); best_a=a; best_M=conv_max(a); best_J=2n best_M.
   - t0.
   - lr = 0.1 adaptive; stall_count=0.
   - beta = beta_start; iterations.
   - Each loop:
      if time > limit break.
      compute q, M = max q. Update best.
      p = softmax(beta*(q - M)); use maybe qn? q small; beta adjusted.
      grad = convolution(p, rev(a)) positions, *2.
      center grad: g = grad - np.mean(grad) (or weighted?)
      scale = np.max(np.abs(g)); if scale<1e-30 break.
      Choose step λ = lr / scale. But maybe lr=1.0 as max log change. Use λ0 = step_base / (np.linalg.norm(g,2)?). Let's use max: max_log_change = 0.5. λ = max_log_change / max_abs_g (after centering). Then backtracking:
         base_log = np.log(a + eps)
         for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]:
             step = λ*factor
             lognew = base_log - step*g
             clip lognew to [logmin, logmax] maybe.
             a_new = exp(lognew - max); normalize.
             M_new = conv_max(a_new)
             if M_new < M - 1e-13: accept, best, break.
             else: if M_new < M + tolerance and soft_new < soft? The soft value for current: soft = M + (1/beta)*log(mean exp(beta(q-M)))? If p sum=exp(beta(q-M)), mean = p.sum()/L. soft = M + (1/beta) log(p.sum()/L). For trial, could compute q_new, M_new, soft_new. We can compute q_new anyway for conv max. If M_new not improve but soft_new < soft, maybe accept with small factor? But to avoid drift, require factor <0.25 maybe. Simpler: accept only if M_new <= M - 1e-15 or (M_new < M + 1e-10 and soft_new < soft - 1e-12). But line search max iterations; if none, reduce lr.
         If no accept: lr *= 0.7; stall++; if stall > ... restart perturb; if lr too small, restart.
      If accept: reset stall maybe, adapt lr increase if many accept: lr = min(lr*1.2, lr_max). beta increase slowly: beta = min(beta*1.02, beta_max).
   - Restarts: if stall>stall_limit, perturb best or current with random noise/mix parametric; reset a,best? Keep global best.
   - Periodically (e.g. every 500 it) try "active-set LP direction" or coordinate moves.

```
def md_optimize(a0, time_limit, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=5000.0, lr_start=0.5, max_change=0.7, restart_stall=500, verbose=True):
    n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1(a0); N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    q, M = conv_and_max(a,N); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=2*n*M
    t0=time.monotonic(); beta=beta_start; lr=lr_start; stall=0; last_save=time...
    base_log_cache? We can recompute log(a+eps) each iter; cheap.
    for it in range(...):
       if time.time - t0 > time_limit: break
       # update every 20 iters print
       # if M < best_M - ... update
       # compute p, note p sum before normalization for soft
       z=beta*(q-M); p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); p_norm=p/psum
       g2 = 2*correlation(p_norm,a,N)  # p_norm sum1
       g = g2 - np.mean(g2)  # optional
       scale = max(abs(g)); if scale<... break
       lam = max_change / scale
       # To avoid huge step due to outliers, use median absolute? maybe max.
       accepted=False
       # try factors descending: 1,0.7,0.5,0.3125,... 10 times
       base_log = np.log(a+1e-300)
       for fac in ...:
           step = lam*fac
           lognew = base_log - step*g
           # Avoid overflow: clip lognew to [logmin, logmax]. Since we normalize, can subtract max.
           m = np.max(lognew); new = np.exp(lognew - m); new = np.maximum(new, 1e-300); s=new.sum(); if s<...: continue; new /=s
           q_new, M_new = conv_and_max(new,N)
           if M_new < best_M - 1e-13:
               best_M= M_new; best_a=new.copy(); best_J=2*n*M_new
               a=new; accepted=True; break
           if M_new < M - 1e-14:
               a=new; M=M_new; accepted=True; break
           # maybe accept soft if M_new <= M + 1e-12 and soft_new < soft_old  (but need soft_old)
       if accepted:
           # reset stall partly, maybe increase lr if best improved
           if M < best_M? no
           lr = min(lr*1.15, 5.0)
           beta = min(beta*1.03, beta_end)
           stall=0
       else:
           stall +=1
           lr *=0.6
           if lr < 1e-5 or stall > 300:
               # restart: perturb best
               # mix with random parametric
               a = perturbation(best_a, n, rng, mix=0.1); q,M=...
               stall=0; lr=lr_start; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.5)
   return best_a, best_J
```

def conv_and_max(a, N):
    L=2*len(a)-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:L]
    # clean negatives
    # return c, float(c.max())
```

Restart perturb details top-level:
```
def random_perturb_sequence(a, rng, mix=0.2, n=None):
   # returns new normalized sequence mixing a and random/param
   n=len(a)
   # choose random direction: noise, uniform, linear ramp, random exponential
   choice = rng.integers(0,4)
   if choice==0: f=rng.normal(0,0.1,n)*a + 1e-8? Actually relative: f = a + abs noise*a; then norm.
   elif choice==1: f= (1-mix)*a + mix*make_linear_ramp(n, float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.6)))
   elif choice==2: f= (1-mix)*a + mix*(rng.exponential(1,n))
   else: f= a + rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)*a
   f=np.maximum(f,0); return norm_sum1(f)
```

Destination j with smallest g_j * a_j? For moving mass, the first-order change in soft objective for infinitesimal transfer δ from i to j is δ(g_j - g_i) (since decrease i, increase j). To decrease, want g_j < g_i. For finite, choose top few sources by g_i/a? Since transfer amount proportional a_i. Let score_i = g_i / a_i? Log gradient. Pseudo:
```
def local_pair_search(a0, time_limit, seed, verbose=True):
 n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1(a0+N?); q,M=conv_and_max; best...
 t0...
 rng=default_rng(seed)
 while time < limit:
    # ensure a positive
    a = np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=a.sum()
    q,M = conv_and_max(a,N)
    update best
    # beta for scoring
    beta = 50.0
    z=beta*(q-M); p=exp; p*=1/p.sum()
    g=2*correlation(p,a,N); g -= g.mean()
    pos = np.argsort(g) # low, high
    cand_dest = pos[:10]
    cand_src = pos[::-1]
    # exclude near zero sources
    improved=False
    # mirror random moves
    if not improved:
       for _ in range(20):
           i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=n-1-i; if i!=j:
              for frac in [0.05,0.15,0.3,0.6,1.0]:
                 for direction in [(-,+),(+,-)]: # move from i to j or j to i
   # pair moves from candidates
    for src in cand_src[:8]:
      if a[src]<1e-12: continue
      for dst in cand_dest[:8]:
        if dst==src: continue
        if a[dst] > 0.5*a[src]? maybe still
        for frac in [0.03,0.08,0.15,0.3]:
           delta=frac*a[src]; if delta<... continue
           new=a.copy(); new[src]-=delta; new[dst]+=delta; new=np.maximum(new,0); new/=new.sum()
           qn,Mn=conv_and_max(new,N)
           if Mn < best_M - 1e-14: accept break all
    if not improved:
       # random transfer
       ...
       if many stalls: perturb best
```

But if best_M improves rarely, time spent. We can limit trials per sweep and recompute g less often (e.g. recompute every sweep). Good.

Implement `make_derivative(k,a,idx?)` memory:
```
def derivative_array(k, a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    D=np.zeros(L)
    D[k:k+n] = 2.0*a
    return D
```
This is O(n) allocation. For factor δ, linear contribution δ * (D_j - D_i). D arrays constant until a changes.

We can even update q exactly without full D arrays for candidate pairs by using slices: For source i, dest j, q_new = q + 2δ(a shifted by j - a shifted by i) + sparse. Could compute vector using numpy roll? But D arrays easier.

Local pair search loop details:
```
def exact_pair_polish(a0, time_limit, seed, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=1000.0, verbose=True):
  n=...; a=norm; q,M=conv; best
  t0
  rng
  # beta adaptive
  while time:
    # ensure positive
    a = np.maximum(a,1e-15); a/=a.sum()
    q,M=conv_and_max(a,N); update best
    # choose beta based on current spread? Use list cycles [20,80,300,1000]
    # p softmax, g gradient
    z=beta*(q-M); p=exp; psum; pnorm
    g=2*correlation(pnorm,a,N); g-=g.mean()
    # candidates: sources high positive g*a? Use g_sorted.
    # To avoid low mass sources, weight by a (transfer effect ∝a_i) maybe score = g_i * a_i? Since first-order change for moving mass proportional a_i(g_i - mean). Use `score = a_i * g_i`. Destinations low `a_i*g_i`? But if a_i zero, cannot dest? dest can be zero. For source effect magnitude ∝a_i, so source index with large a_i*g_i. For destination, want low g but mass irrelevant; maybe low g. Use separate.
    src_order = np.argsort(a*g)[::-1]
    dst_order = np.argsort(g + 1e9*(a<1e-12)? maybe)
    # Precompute D for unique top 12 src and top 12 dst.
    D_cache = {}
    for idx in candidates: D_cache[idx]=derivative_array(idx,a)
    trial_count=0; improved=False
    # Also include mirror pairs for high src maybe.
    for s in top_srcs: ... for d in top_dsts + [mirror(s)]
       if s==d: continue
       mass=a[s]
       if mass<1e-14: continue
       deltas = mass * [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0]
       Ds=D_cache[s]; Dj=get(d)
       lin = Dj - Ds
       for δ in deltas:
          tmp = q + δ*lin
          d2=δ*δ
          # adjust scalar: t_ii=2*s, t_jj=2*d, t_ij=s+d
          if t_ii < L: tmp[t_ii] += d2

Function `apply_trial(a,q,s,d,δ,N?)` top-level:
```
def trial_transfer_max(q, a, s, d, delta, D_s=None, D_d=None):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if D_s is None: D_s=derivative_array(s,a)
    if D_d is None: D_d=derivative_array(d,a)
    tmp = q + delta*(D_d-D_s)
    d2=delta*delta
    t1=2*s; t2=2*d; t3=s+d
    tmp[t1] += d2
    tmp[t2] += d2
    tmp[t3] -= 2*d2
    return float(tmp.max()), tmp
```

Implement `correlation_p_a(p,a,N)`:
```
def correlation_p_a(p,a,N):
    n=len(a)
    Pa=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    Arev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Pa*Arev,N)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n
```
Check slice end: n-1 index start, need n elements: start=n-1, end=2*n-1 exclusive? Since indices 0..2n-2 inclusive? L_grad length L+n-1=3n-2, valid positions n-1 .. 2n-2 inclusive length n. End = n-1+n = 2n-1. Python slice `conv[n-1:2*n-1]`. For n=2 start1 end3 => indices1,2 good. Use `2*n-1`.

def random_perturb_sequence(a, rng, mix_low=0.15, mix_high=0.45):
    n=len(a)
    mix=float(rng.uniform(mix_low, mix_high))
    choice=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if choice==0: f=rng.normal(0.0,0.1,n); f=f*f? Actually multiplicatively: f = a * np.maximum(1.0 + 0.2*rng.normal(0,1,n),0.1)
    elif 1: shape=make_linear_ramp(n, rng.uniform(0.3,0.6)); f=(1-mix)*a+mix*shape
    elif 2: shape=make_exp_shape(n, rng.uniform(-2,2), rng.uniform(-1,1)); f=...
    elif 3: shape=make_gaussian_pair(n, rng.uniform(0.3,0.5), rng.uniform(0.7,0.8), rng.uniform(0.08,0.25)); f=...
    else: shape=norm_sum1(rng.exponential(1,n)); f=(1-mix)*a+mix*shape
    return norm_sum1(f)
```

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    a = a/s
    # add tiny floor? maybe only for optimization
    a = a + 1e-15
    a = a/a.sum()
    return a
```
Adding floor changes score slightly. If score final, 1e-15 uniform no effect. But for n large, floor total 5e-12, negligible. Good. However if some entries should be zero, floor adds mass; okay. For exact pair, a_i may be floor; source mass non-zero. Good.

`conv_and_max(a, N=None)`:
```
def conv_and_max(a, N=None):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
    # optional max = float(np.max(c)); if c has negative due round
    return c, float(c.max())
```
For gradient N maybe larger; if call conv with N larger than min, okay. But `conv_and_max` expects N; we can use N_global. In pair polish exact no FFT after initial? It uses q from conv_and_max with N. Good.

`soft_value(q,beta)`:
```
def soft_value(q,beta):
    M=float(q.max())
    if beta <=0: return M
    z=beta*(q-M)
    s=float(np.exp(z).sum())
    if s <=0: return M
    return float(M + (np.log(s) - np.log(q.size))/beta)
```
In MD, we may need current soft. Use psum from softmax: `psum` before normalization. `soft = M + (log(psum)-log(L))/beta`.

x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
f=1.0 + r*(1.0-2.0*x)
f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
return norm_sum1(f)
```

`md_optimize(a0, time_limit, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=5000.0, lr_start=0.6, max_log_change=0.6, seed=1, verbose=True)`:
Implement as discussed. Need access to `random_perturb_sequence`. Use `time.monotonic`.
Pseudo code complete:
```
def md_optimize(...):
    n=a0.size; a=norm_sum1(a0); N=next_pow2(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
    q,M=conv_and_max(a,N); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=2*n*M
    t0=time.monotonic(); beta=beta_start; lr=0.6; stall=0; it=0
    eps=1e-15
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it +=1
       a = np.maximum(a, eps); s=a.sum(); if s!=1: a/=s; q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
       if M < best_M - 1e-14: update
       z=beta*(q-M); p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); if psum<=0: break? p=p/L; psum=L
       p_norm = p / psum
       g = 2.0 * p_a_correlation(p_norm,a,N)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.zeros(n)
       g = np.nan_to_num(g)
       g_center = g - float(np.mean(g))
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g_center)))
       if scale < 1e-12:
           # perturb restart
           a = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, np.random.default_rng(seed+it), 0.15, 0.35)
           q,M=conv_and_max(a,N); stall=0; beta=max(beta_start,beta*0.7); continue
       lam = min(max_log_change / scale, 1e5)
       accepted=False
       base_log = np.log(a + 1e-300)
       soft_old = M + (np.log(psum) - np.log(L)) / beta
       for fac in MD_FACTOR:
           step = lam*fac
           lognew = base_log - step*g_center
           maxlog = float(np.max(lognew))
           new = np.exp(lognew - maxlog)
           new = np.maximum(new,0)
           snew=new.sum()
           if snew <=0: continue
           new /= snew
           q_new, M_new = conv_and_max(new,N)
           if M_new < best_M - 1e-14:
               best_M=M_new; best_a=new.copy(); best_J=2*n*M_new
               a=new; M=M_new; accepted=True; break
           if M_new < M - 1e-14:
               a=new; M=M_new; accepted=True; break
           # accept if same max but soft decreased significantly
           if M_new <= M + 1e-12:
               # compute soft_new maybe use p? Since p weights old, approximate linear? Use simple: M_new and mean of top? skip
               pass
       if accepted:
           if M < best_M - 1e-16? already
           lr = min(2.0, lr*1.1)
           beta = min(beta_end, beta*1.02)
           stall=0
       else:
           stall +=1
           lr = max(0.05, lr*0.7)
           if stall > 200 or lr < 0.05:
               seed_use = seed + it
               a = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, np.random.default_rng(seed_use), 0.15, 0.5)
               q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
               stall=0; lr=0.6; beta=max(beta_start, beta*0.8)
       if verbose and it % 200 ==0:
           elapsed=...
           print(...)
    # final maybe ensure best
    return best_a, best_J
```

def exact_pair_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=True):
    n=a0.size; a=norm_sum1(a0); N=next_pow2(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
    q,M=conv_and_max(a,N); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=2*n*M
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=default_rng(seed); no_imp=0; beta_idx=0
    beta_cycle=(20,50,100,200,500,1000)
    anneal = 1e-9? always 0? maybe allow small worsening: accept if M_tr <= M + 1e-12 + anneal and obj_change < -...
    while time < time_limit:
        a = np.maximum(a,1e-15); s=a.sum(); if abs(s-1)>1e-12: a/=s; q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
        if M < best_M - 1e-14: update
        beta=beta_cycle[beta_idx % len]
        z=beta*(q-M); p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); p /= psum
        g = 2*p_a_correlation(p,a,N)
        # candidate lists
        src_order = np.argsort(a*g)[::-1]  # coordinates with positive effect? Could be negative g and large a -> high source? We want high g not multiplied? If g can be negative, a*g ordering maybe weird. Use np.argsort(g * (a>1e-12)). For source, coefficient magnitude and mass: first-order O change for moving all mass from i: a_i*(g_d-g_i). To reduce, we want high g_i large a_i. Use a_i*g_i.
        dst_order = np.argsort(g)
        srcs = src_order[:12]
        dsts = dst_order[:12]
        # add mirror of srcs
        cand = list unique
        D_cache = {idx: derivative_array(idx,a) for idx in cand if 0<=idx<n}
        improved=False
        # candidate destinations for each source: dsts + mirror(s)
        for idx_s in srcs:
            s_idx=int(idx_s)
            mass=a[s_idx]
            if mass < 1e-13: continue
            Ds=D_cache.get(s_idx) or derivative_array(s_idx,a)
            dests=set(dsts); dests.add(n-1-s_idx)
            for d_idx in dests:
                d_idx=int(d_idx)
                if d_idx == s_idx: continue
                if a[d_idx]<0? no
                Dj=D_cache.get(d_idx) or derivative_array(d_idx,a)
                lin = Dj - Ds
                # first obj sign: g[d]-g[s]
                gd = float(g[d_idx]); gs=float(g[s_idx])
                # if gd >= gs + 1e-12 maybe still second order? skip if no mass? But for mirror with equal, skip; random later.
                if gd > gs and float(p[2*d_idx]+p[2*s_idx]-2*p[s_idx+d_idx])*mass*mass >? Hard. Skip if gd - gs > 1e-12 (positive) maybe.
                for frac in _PAIR_FRACTIONS:
                    delta = mass*frac
                    if delta <= 1e-18: continue
                    obj_change = delta*(gd-gs) + delta*delta*(p[2*d_idx]+p[2*s_idx]-2*p[s_idx+d_idx])
                    # skip if obj_change too positive unless force? Use if obj_change > 1e-15: maybe try only for small fractions? To save, continue.
                    if obj_change > 1e-13 and frac > 0.05: continue
                    Mtr = trial_transfer_max(q, a, s_idx, d_idx, delta, Ds, Dj)
                    accept = False
                    if Mtr < best_M - 1e-15:
                        accept=True
                    elif Mtr < M - 1e-15:
                        accept=True
                    elif Mtr <= M + 1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-16:
                        accept=True
                    # annealing accept? if Mtr < best_M + 1e-8 or M + 1e-9 and rng.random<0.1? maybe not.
                    if accept:
                        a_new = a.copy(); a_new[s_idx] -= delta; a_new[d_idx] += delta
                        a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
                        sumn = a_new.sum(); if abs(sumn-1)>1e-12: a_new/=sumn
                        q_new = q + delta*(Dj-Ds) # allocate again? Could use trial return; modify to return tmp.
                        # Need avoid recomputing vector? Make trial return tmp too.
```

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(float(budget_s)-60.0, 1740.0)
    # load parent
    best_a=None; best_score=inf

Pseudo full exact:
```
def exact_pair_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=True):
  n=...; a=norm_sum1; N=next_pow2(3*n-2); L=2*n-1
  q,M=conv_and_max(a,N); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=2*n*M
  t0=...; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); no_imp=0; sweep=0; beta=20.0; best_print=best_M
  while time < time_limit:
    sweep +=1
    if sweep % 100 ==0:
       # recompute q to correct drift
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); s=a.sum(); if not close: a/=s
       q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
    if M < best_M -1e-14: update
    # adaptive beta
    beta = min(3000.0, max(20.0, beta*1.01)) maybe
    z=beta*(q-M); p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); p/=psum
    g=2*p_a_correlation(p,a,N); g=np.nan_to_num(g)
    src_score=a*g; srcs=np.argsort(src_score)[::-1][:12]; dsts=np.argsort(g)[:12]
    cand=set(...)
    D_cache={}
    for idx in cand: if idx>=0 and idx<n: D_cache[idx]=derivative_array(int(a),a)
    improved=False
    accepted_move=False
    for si in srcs:
       if not accepted_move:
         s_i=int(si); mass=a[s_i]; if mass<1e-13: continue
         Ds=D_cache.get(s_i, derivative_array(s_i,a))
         # dest list
         dests = [int(d) for d in dsts]
         mir=n-1-s_i
         if mir not in dests: dests.append(mir)
         for di in dests:
            if accepted_move: break
            if di==s_i or di<0 or di>=n: continue
            Dj=D_cache.get(di, derivative_array(di,a))
            lin=Dj-Ds
            gd=float(g[di]); gs=float(g[s_i])
            # skip if first-order clearly increasing and not very small? Use threshold.
            for frac in _PAIR_FRACTIONS:
               delta=mass*frac
               if delta<=1e-18: continue
               d2=delta*delta
               # small linear cost estimate:
               obj1=delta*(gd-gs)
               if obj1 > 1e-12:
                   # maybe still second order could reduce max; only try if frac<=0.1
                   if frac > 0.1: continue
               obj_change=obj1 + d2*(p[2*di]+p[2*si]-2*p[si+di])
               Mtr,tmp=trial_transfer(q,a,si,di,delta,Ds,Dj)
               accept=False
               if Mtr < best_M - 1e-15: accept=True
               elif Mtr < M - 1e-15: accept=True
               elif Mtr <= M + 1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-18: accept=True
               if accept:
                   a_new=a.copy(); a_new[si]-=delta; a_new[di]+=delta; a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
                   sumn=a_new.sum(); scale=1.0/sumn if sumn>0 else 1.0
                   if abs(scale-1)>1e-12:
                       a_new*=scale; tmp*=scale*scale
                   a=a_new; q=tmp
                   M=float(q.max())
                   if M < best_M:
                       best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); best_J=2*n*M
                   improved=True; accepted_move=True; break
    if not improved:
       # random moves 30
       for _ in range(30):
          # choose source/dest using g maybe
          sidx=int(rng.integers(n)); didx=int(rng.integers(n))
          if sidx==didx: continue
          if g[didx] < g[sidx]: source=sidx; dest=didx
          else: source=didx; dest=sidx
          mass=a[source]
          if mass<1e-13: continue
          Ds=derivative_array(source,a); Dj=derivative_array(dest,a); lin=Dj-Ds
          gd=float(g[dest]); gs=float(g[source])
          for frac in (0.05,0.15,0.3,0.6):
             delta=mass*frac
             Mtr,tmp=trial_transfer(...)
             obj_change=delta*(gd-gs)+...
             if Mtr < best_M -1e-15 or Mtr < M -1e-15 or (Mtr <= M+1e-12 and obj_change< -1e-18): accept
             if accept: update...
          if improved: break
    if improved:
       no_imp=0
       beta=min(5000.0, beta*1.05)
    else:
       no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > 40:
           # perturb from best
           a=random_perturb_sequence(best_a,rng,0.15,0.45)
           q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
           no_imp=0
           beta=50.0
    if verbose and (improved or sweep%50==0): print
  return best_a,best_J
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(x)))
```
But `np.log2` could produce float; for x integer, use bit_length:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<0.01 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf
    a=a/s
    N=next_pow2(3*a.size-2) # can use larger? for score only need 2 but okay? Use 2 to save: next_pow2(2*n-1)
    _, M=conv_and_max(a, next_pow2(2*a.size-1))
    return float(2*a.size*M)
```
But `conv_and_max` uses N; for n=5200 next pow 16384. Good. If use N=3n, extra cost. Use 2n.

Now code helpers:

`make_power`:
```
def make_power(n, p, q):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    eps=0.05
    f=np.power(x+eps, p) * np.power((1.0-x)+eps, q)
    return norm_sum1(f)
```

`make_cheb`:
```
def make_cheb(n, c1, c2):
   u=2*linspace(0,1,n)-1
   t1=u; t3=4*u*u*u-3*u
   z=c1*t1+c2*t3
   z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
   return norm_sum1(np.exp(z))
```

`make_gauss_pair`:
```
def make_gauss_pair(n,c1,c2,s1,s2):
   x=...; f=exp(-((x-c1)^2)/(2*s1*s1))+exp(...); return norm_sum1(f)
```

`random_perturb_sequence` uses these top-level.
```
def random_perturb_sequence(a, rng, mix_low=0.15, mix_high=0.4):
    n=a.size
    mix=float(rng.uniform(mix_low,mix_high))
    choice=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if choice ==0:
       f=a*np.maximum(1.0+0.3*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n),0.1)
    elif choice ==1:
       shape=make_linear_ramp(n,float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.7)))
       f=(1.0-mix)*a+mix*shape
    elif choice ==2:
       shape=make_exp_shape(n,float(rng.uniform(-3.0,3.0)), float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)))
       f=(1.0-mix)*a+mix*shape
    elif choice ==3:
       shape=make_gauss_pair(n,float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.45)),float(rng.uniform(0.55,0.75)),float(rng.uniform(0.08,0.25)),float(rng.uniform(0.08,0.25)))
       f=(1.0-mix)*a+mix*shape
    else:
       shape=norm_sum1(rng.exponential(1.0,n))
       f=(1.0-mix)*a+mix*shape
    return norm_sum1(f)
```
Need `rng` type np.random.Generator; methods used.

`interpolate_sequence` handle old_n==1:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    old_n=a.size
    if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
    if old_n <=1:
       # keep mass? uniform? If old sequence [1], interpolated maybe ones(new_n)/new_n
       return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n); new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    f=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return norm_sum1(f)
```

Now, `md_optimize` details. Let's write robust.
```
def md_optimize(a0, time_limit, seed=1, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=5000.0, verbose=True):
    n=len(a0)
    if n==0: return None, inf
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    L=2*n-1
    q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=float(2*n*M)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    beta=float(beta_start)
    stall=0
    it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while not (time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit):
        it +=1
        # periodic recompute? a updated via FFT each line, q from current maybe small drift; recompute every 500
        if it % 500 ==0:
            a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); s=a.sum(); if abs(s-1)>1e-12: a/=s
            q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); best_J=float(2*n*M)
        z=beta*(q-M)
        p=np.exp(z)
        psum=float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(psum) or psum <=0:
            p=np.ones(L)/L; psum=1.0
        else:
            p=p/psum
        g=2.0*p_a_correlation(p,a,N)
        g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gc=g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
        if scale < 1e-12:
            # finite random perturb
            a=random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
            q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
            stall=0
            beta=max(float(beta_start), beta*0.8)
            continue
        lam=min(0.7/scale, 1e5)
        base_log=np.log(a + 1e-300)
        accepted=False
        for fac in _MD_FACTORS:
            step=lam*fac
            lognew=base_log - step*gc
            mx=float(np.max(lognew))
            if not np.isfinite(mx): continue
            new=np.exp(lognew-mx)
            new=np.nan_to_num(new, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            snew=float(new.sum())
            if snew <= 1e-300: continue
            new=new/snew
            q_new,M_new=conv_and_max(new,N)
            if M_new < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M=M_new; best_a=new.copy(); best_J=float(2*n*M_new)
                a=new; M=M_new; accepted=True; break
            if M_new < M - 1e-14:
                a=new; M=M_new; accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
            stall=0
            beta=min(float(beta_end), beta*1.03)
        else:
            stall +=1
            if stall > 150:
                a=random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
                q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
                stall=0
                beta=max(float(beta_start), beta*0.8)
        if verbose and it % 200 ==0:
            el=time.monotonic()-t0
            print(f"    MD iter={it} t={el:.0f} J_best={best_J:.6f} beta={beta:.1f} stall={stall}", flush=True)
    return best_a, best_J
```
Potential: If beta gets too large, z differences small? Actually large focuses. psum could be 1 (top only). Fine. If p is top only, g may be constant in symmetric causing scale small -> perturb.

Now, exact_pair_polish code. Need top-level `trial_transfer` and `derivative_array`.

`trial_transfer`:
```
def trial_transfer(q, a, s, d, delta, Ds, Dj):
    tmp = q + delta * (Dj - Ds)
    d2 = delta*delta
    t1=2*s; t2=2*d; t3=s+d
    if 0 <= t1 < tmp.size: tmp[t1] += d2
    if 0 <= t2 < tmp.size: tmp[t2] += d2
    if 0 <= t3 < tmp.size: tmp[t3] -= 2.0*d2
    return float(np.max(tmp)), tmp
```
Note if t1==t2? only s=d not; if t1==t3, operations add then subtract; coefficient -1 correct. But in Python if same index two times, first tmp[t1]+=d2 then tmp[t3]-=2d2; if t1==t3, net -d2 correct. If t2 same t3, similarly. If all same (s=d) not. Good.

`exact_pair_polish` full. Need careful with loops and break.

Implement with `improved_any` flag and nested loops. Use `break` with a label? Python no; use flags.

```
def exact_pair_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=True):
    n=...; a=norm; N...; q,M...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_J=...
    t0; rng; no_imp=0; sweep=0; beta=30.0
    while time < time_limit:
        sweep +=1
        if sweep % 200 ==0:
           a=np.maximum(a,1e-15); s=a.sum(); if abs(s-1)>1e-12: a/=s
           q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
        if M < best_M -1e-14: update
        beta = min(5000.0, max(20.0, beta*1.01))
        z=beta*(q-M); p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); ... p/=psum
        g=2.0*p_a_correlation(p,a,N); g=np.nan_to_num(g)
        src_score = a * g
        srcs = np.argsort(src_score)[::-1][:12]
        dsts = np.argsort(g)[:12]
        cand=set(int(i) for i in srcs)
        cand.update(int(i) for i in dsts)
        for s_i in srcs:
            cand.add(int(n-1-int(s_i)))
        D_cache={}
        for idx in cand:
           if 0 <= idx < n:
              D_cache[idx]=derivative_array(idx,a)
        improved=False
        # helper accept_idx? no closure. We'll update directly and set improved.
        for si in srcs:
            if improved: break
            si=int(si); mass=float(a[si])
            if mass < 1e-13: continue
            Ds=D_cache.get(si, derivative_array(si,a))
            dests=[]
            for di in dsts:
               intd=int(di)
               if intd not in dests: dests.append(intd)
            mir=n-1-si
            if mir not in dests: dests.append(mir)
            gs=float(g[si]); D? 
            for di in dests:
               if improved: break
               if di==si: continue
               Dj=D_cache.get(di, derivative_array(di,a))
               lin=Dj-Ds
               gd=float(g[di])
               for frac in _PAIR_FRACTIONS:
                  delta=mass*frac
                  if delta <=1e-18: continue
                  d2=delta*delta
                  # objective first approx
                  obj1=delta*(gd-gs)
                  if obj1 > 1e-10 and frac > 0.1:
                      continue
                  Mtr,tmp=trial_transfer(q,a,si,di,delta,Ds,Dj)
                  obj_change=obj1 + d2*(p[2*di]+p[2*si]-2.0*p[si+di])
                  accept=False
                  if Mtr < best_M - 1e-15: accept=True
                  elif Mtr < M - 1e-15: accept=True
                  elif Mtr <= M + 1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-18: accept=True
                  if accept:
                      a_new=a.copy(); a_new[si]-=delta; a_new[di]+=delta; a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
                      sumn=float(a_new.sum())
                      if sumn <=1e-300: continue
                      scale=1.0/sumn
                      if abs(scale-1.0) > 1e-12:
                          a_new *= scale; tmp *= (scale*scale)
                      a=a_new; q=tmp
                      M=float(q.max())
                      if M < best_M:
                          best_M=M; best_a=a.copy(); best_J=float(2*n*M)
                      improved=True; break
        if improved:
            no_imp=0
        else:
            # random moves
            for _ in range(40):
                sidx=int(rng.integers(n)); didx=int(rng.integers(n))
                if sidx == didx: continue
                if g[sidx] >= g[didx]: source=sidx; dest=didx
                else: source=didx; dest=sidx
                mass=float(a[source])
                if mass < 1e-13: continue
                Ds=derivative_array(source,a); Dj=derivative_array(dest,a)
                gs=float(g[source]); gd=float(g[dest])
                for frac in _RANDOM_FRACTIONS:
                    delta=mass*frac
                    if delta<=1e-18: continue
                    Mtr,tmp=trial_transfer(q,a,source,dest,delta,Ds,Dj)
                    d2=delta*delta
                    obj_change=delta*(gd-gs)+d2*(p[2*dest]+p[2*source]-2.0*p[source+dest])
                    accept=False
                    if Mtr < best_M -1e-15 or Mtr < M -1e-15 or (Mtr <= M+1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-18): accept=True
                    if accept:
                       # update same
                        ...
                       improved=True; break
                if improved: break
            if not improved:
                no_imp +=1
                if no_imp > 60:
                    a=random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
                    q,M=conv_and_max(a,N)
                    no_imp=0
                    beta=50.0
        if verbose and (improved or sweep % 50 ==0):
            el=time.monotonic()-t0
            print(f"    PairPoly sweep={sweep} t={el:.0f} J_best={best_J:.6f} no_imp={no_imp}", flush=True)
    return best_a, best_J
```
Need ensure after random update we set improved and break; the code duplication for update can be factored into top-level helper? No closures but can function `apply_pair_update(a,q,si,di,delta,tmp,return?)` returning new a,q,M. Let's create top-level:
```
def apply_pair_update(a, q, s, d, delta, tmp):
    a_new = a.copy(); a_new[s] -= delta; a_new[d] += delta; a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
    sumn = float(a_new.sum())
    if sumn <= 0: return a, q, float(q.max())
    scale = 1.0/sumn
    if abs(scale-1.0) > 1e-12:
        a_new *= scale; tmp *= scale*scale
    q_new = np.maximum(tmp,0.0)
    M_new = float(q_new.max())
    return a_new, q_new, M_new
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing the given evaluation function.

The implementation combines:
  - a direct FFT-based mirror-descent step on a log-sum-exp approximation,
  - an exact fast pair-transfer local search using O(n) convolution updates,
  - several asymmetric parametric starting points and random restarts.

All helper functions are top-level; propose_candidate() is the entry point.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_MD_FACTORS = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.18, 0.13, 0.09, 0.065)
_PAIR_FRACTIONS = (0.01, 0.025, 0.05, 0.1, 0.18, 0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
_RANDOM_FRACTIONS = (0.05, 0.15, 0.3, 0.6)


def next_pow2(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    a = a / s
    # keep a tiny floor to protect log updates and exact pair search
    a = a + 1e-15
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def consider_candidate(a, current_a, current_score):
    try:
        c = norm_sum1(a)
        sc = score_normalized(c)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score:
            return c, sc
    except Exception:
        pass
    return current_a, current_score


def make_linear_ramp(n, r):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = 1.0 + float(r) * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    return norm_sum1(f)


def make_exp_shape(n, a, b):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = float(a) * x + float(b) * x * x
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    return norm_sum1(np.exp(z))


def make_power(n, p, q):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = 0.05
    f = np.power(x + eps, float(p)) * np.power((1.0 - x) + eps, float(q))
    return norm_sum1(f)


def make_cheb(n, c1, c2):
    u = 2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n) - 1.0
    t1 = u
    t3 = 4.0 * u * u * u - 3.0 * u
    z = float(c1) * t1 + float(c2) * t3
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    return norm_sum1(np.exp(z))


def make_gauss_pair(n, c1, c2, s1, s2):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    c1 = float(c1)
    c2 = float(c2)
    s1 = max(1e-4, float(s1))
    s2 = max(1e-4, float(s2))
    f = np.exp(-((x - c1) ** 2) / (2.0 * s1 * s1)) + np.exp(-((x - c2) ** 2) / (2.0 * s2 * s2))
    return norm_sum1(f)


def random_perturb_sequence(a, rng, mix_low=0.15, mix_high=0.4):
    n = a.size
    mix = float(rng.uniform(mix_low, mix_high))
    choice = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
    if choice == 0:
        f = a * np.maximum(1.0 + 0.3 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n), 0.1)
    elif choice == 1:
        shape = make_linear_ramp(n, rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        f = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * shape
    elif choice == 2:
        shape = make_exp_shape(n, rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0), rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        f = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * shape
    elif choice == 3:
        shape = make_gauss_pair(
            n,
            rng.uniform(0.25, 0.45),
            rng.uniform(0.55, 0.75),
            rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25),
            rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25),
        )
        f = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * shape
    else:
        shape = norm_sum1(rng.exponential(1.0, n))
        f = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * shape
    return norm_sum1(f)


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    old_n = a.size
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n <= 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    f = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    return norm_sum1(f)


def conv_and_max(a, N=None):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None or N < L:
        N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, float(c.max())


def score_normalized(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    N = next_pow2(2 * a.size - 1)
    _, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
    return float(2 * a.size * M)


def p_a_correlation(p, a, N=None):
    """
    Given p length 2n-1 and a length n, returns length-n vector r where
    r[i] = sum_t p[t] * a[t-i] (out-of-range terms zero).
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None or N < L + n - 1:
        N = next_pow2(L + n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    Arev = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * Arev, N)
    return c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def derivative_array(k, a):
    """
    D_k[t] = d conv(a,a)[t] / d a_k = 2*a[t-k] if t-k in [0,n-1], else 0.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    D = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    D[k:k + n] = 2.0 * a
    return D


def trial_transfer(q, a, s, d, delta, Ds, Dj):
    """
    Exact convolution update after moving mass delta from index s to index d.
    Returns (new_max, updated_convolution_array).
    """
    tmp = q + delta * (Dj - Ds)
    d2 = delta * delta
    t1 = 2 * s
    t2 = 2 * d
    t3 = s + d
    if 0 <= t1 < tmp.size:
        tmp[t1] += d2
    if 0 <= t2 < tmp.size:
        tmp[t2] += d2
    if 0 <= t3 < tmp.size:
        tmp[t3] -= 2.0 * d2
    return float(np.max(tmp)), tmp


def apply_pair_update(a, q, s, d, delta, tmp):
    a_new = a.copy()
    a_new[s] -= delta
    a_new[d] += delta
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    sum_new = float(a_new.sum())
    if sum_new <= 1e-300:
        return a, q, float(q.max())
    scale = 1.0 / sum_new
    if abs(scale - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a_new *= scale
        tmp *= scale * scale
    q_new = np.maximum(tmp, 0.0)
    m_new = float(q_new.max())
    return a_new, q_new, m_new


def md_optimize(a0, time_limit, seed=1, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=5000.0, verbose=True):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    n = a0.size
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    best_J = float(2 * n * M)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    beta = float(beta_start)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1

        if it % 500 == 0:
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                a = a / s
            q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)

        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_J = float(2 * n * M)

        z = beta * (q - M)
        p = np.exp(z)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(psum) or psum <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            p = p / psum

        g = 2.0 * p_a_correlation(p, a, N)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gc = g - float(np.mean(g))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-12:
            a = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
            q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
            stall = 0
            beta = max(float(beta_start), beta * 0.8)
            continue

        lam = min(0.7 / scale, 1e5)
        base_log = np.log(a + 1e-300)
        accepted = False

        for fac in _MD_FACTORS:
            step = lam * fac
            lognew = base_log - step * gc
            mx = float(np.max(lognew))
            if not np.isfinite(mx):
                continue
            new = np.exp(lognew - mx)
            new = np.nan_to_num(new, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            snew = float(new.sum())
            if snew <= 1e-300:
                continue
            new = new / snew
            q_new, M_new = conv_and_max(new, N)

            if M_new < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = M_new
                best_a = new.copy()
                best_J = float(2 * n * M_new)
                a = new
                M = M_new
                accepted = True
                break
            if M_new < M - 1e-14:
                a = new
                M = M_new
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.03)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 150:
                a = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
                q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
                stall = 0
                beta = max(float(beta_start), beta * 0.8)

        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    MD iter={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s J_best={best_J:.6f} "
                f"beta={beta:.1f} stall={stall}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best_a, best_J


def exact_pair_polish(a0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=True):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a0.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    n = a0.size
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    best_J = float(2 * n * M)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_imp = 0
    sweep = 0
    beta = 30.0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        sweep += 1

        if sweep % 200 == 0:
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                a = a / s
            q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)

        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_J = float(2 * n * M)

        beta = min(5000.0, max(20.0, beta * 1.01))
        z = beta * (q - M)
        p = np.exp(z)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(psum) or psum <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            p = p / psum

        g = 2.0 * p_a_correlation(p, a, N)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        src_score = a * g
        srcs = np.argsort(src_score)[::-1][:12]
        dsts = np.argsort(g)[:12]

        cand = set(int(i) for i in srcs)
        cand.update(int(i) for i in dsts)
        for idx in srcs:
            cand.add(int(n - 1 - int(idx)))

        D_cache = {}
        for idx in cand:
            if 0 <= idx < n:
                D_cache[idx] = derivative_array(idx, a)

        improved = False

        for si in srcs:
            if improved:
                break
            si = int(si)
            mass = float(a[si])
            if mass < 1e-13:
                continue
            Ds = D_cache.get(si, derivative_array(si, a))
            gs = float(g[si])

            dests = [int(d) for d in dsts]
            mir = n - 1 - si
            if mir not in dests:
                dests.append(mir)

            for di in dests:
                if improved:
                    break
                if di == si or di < 0 or di >= n:
                    continue
                Dj = D_cache.get(di, derivative_array(di, a))
                lin = Dj - Ds
                gd = float(g[di])

                for frac in _PAIR_FRACTIONS:
                    delta = mass * frac
                    if delta <= 1e-18:
                        continue
                    d2 = delta * delta

                    # Cheap filtered lookahead: if the first-order weighted
                    # objective is clearly increasing, skip large jumps.
                    obj1 = delta * (gd - gs)
                    if obj1 > 1e-10 and frac > 0.1:
                        continue

                    Mtr, tmp = trial_transfer(q, a, si, di, delta, Ds, Dj)
                    obj_change = obj1 + d2 * (p[2 * di] + p[2 * si] - 2.0 * p[si + di])

                    accept = False
                    if Mtr < best_M - 1e-15:
                        accept = True
                    elif Mtr < M - 1e-15:
                        accept = True
                    elif Mtr <= M + 1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-18:
                        accept = True

                    if accept:
                        a_new, q_new, M_new = apply_pair_update(a, q, si, di, delta, tmp)
                        a = a_new
                        q = q_new
                        M = M_new
                        if M < best_M:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            best_J = float(2 * n * M)
                        improved = True
                        break

        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            # Random / gradient-guided pair moves to escape flat plateaus.
            for _ in range(40):
                sidx = int(rng.integers(n))
                didx = int(rng.integers(n))
                if sidx == didx:
                    continue

                if float(g[sidx]) >= float(g[didx]):
                    source = sidx
                    dest = didx
                else:
                    source = didx
                    dest = sidx

                mass = float(a[source])
                if mass < 1e-13:
                    continue

                Ds = derivative_array(source, a)
                Dj = derivative_array(dest, a)
                gs = float(g[source])
                gd = float(g[dest])

                for frac in _RANDOM_FRACTIONS:
                    delta = mass * frac
                    if delta <= 1e-18:
                        continue
                    d2 = delta * delta

                    Mtr, tmp = trial_transfer(q, a, source, dest, delta, Ds, Dj)
                    obj_change = delta * (gd - gs) + d2 * (
                        p[2 * dest] + p[2 * source] - 2.0 * p[source + dest]
                    )

                    accept = False
                    if Mtr < best_M - 1e-15:
                        accept = True
                    elif Mtr < M - 1e-15:
                        accept = True
                    elif Mtr <= M + 1e-12 and obj_change < -1e-18:
                        accept = True

                    if accept:
                        a_new, q_new, M_new = apply_pair_update(a, q, source, dest, delta, tmp)
                        a = a_new
                        q = q_new
                        M = M_new
                        if M < best_M:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                            best_J = float(2 * n * M)
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

            if not improved:
                no_imp += 1
                if no_imp > 60:
                    a = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng, 0.2, 0.5)
                    q, M = conv_and_max(a, N)
                    no_imp = 0
                    beta = 50.0

        if verbose and (improved or sweep % 50 == 0):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    Pair sweep={sweep} t={elapsed:.0f}s J_best={best_J:.6f} "
                f"no_imp={no_imp}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best_a, best_J


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Entry point invoked by the evaluator. Searches within the given wall-clock
    budget and returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    time_budget = min(budget - 60.0, 1740.0)
    if time_budget < 60.0:
        time_budget = max(20.0, budget - 10.0)

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Try to load the previously found sequence.
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum() > 1e-12:
            parent = norm_sum1(p)
            parent_score = score_normalized(parent)
            if np.isfinite(parent_score):
                best_a = parent
                best_score = parent_score
                print(f"Loaded parent: n={parent.size}, score={parent_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_a is None:
        n0 = 5200
        best_a = make_linear_ramp(n0, 0.42)
        best_score = score_normalized(best_a)
        print(f"No parent loaded. Default start n={best_a.size}, score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 0: cheap parametric exploration at current length --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 30:
        n0 = best_a.size
        print(f"Parametric scan n={n0}", flush=True)

        for r in (0.25, 0.32, 0.38, 0.44, 0.52, 0.62):
            best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(make_linear_ramp(n0, r), best_a, best_score)

        for aa in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            for bb in (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0):
                best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(make_exp_shape(n0, aa, bb), best_a, best_score)

        for pp in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            for qq in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(make_power(n0, pp, qq), best_a, best_score)

        for cc1 in (-1.2, -0.6, 0.6, 1.2):
            for cc2 in (-0.8, 0.0, 0.8):
                best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(make_cheb(n0, cc1, cc2), best_a, best_score)

        for c1 in (0.3, 0.4):
            for c2 in (0.65, 0.75):
                for ss in (0.1, 0.16, 0.25):
                    best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(
                        make_gauss_pair(n0, c1, c2, ss, ss), best_a, best_score
                    )

        print(f"After parametric scan: best score={best_score:.6f}, n={best_a.size}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 1: mirror descent on best sequence --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t1 = min(remaining * 0.30, 480.0)
    if t1 > 20 and best_score > 1.0:
        print(f"Phase 1: MD, budget={t1:.0f}s, n={best_a.size}", flush=True)
        a_out, j_out = md_optimize(best_a, t1, seed=101, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=3000.0, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
            best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
            best_score = float(j_out)
            print(f"MD improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 2: exact pair polish --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    t2 = min(remaining * 0.30, 520.0)
    if t2 > 20 and best_score > 1.0:
        print(f"Phase 2: exact pair polish, budget={t2:.0f}s, n={best_a.size}", flush=True)
        a_out, j_out = exact_pair_polish(best_a, t2, seed=202, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
            best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
            best_score = float(j_out)
            print(f"Pair improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 3: perturbed trajectory --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 80:
        t3 = min(remaining * 0.20, 260.0)
        if t3 > 20 and best_score > 1.0:
            print(f"Phase 3: perturbed MD, budget={t3:.0f}s", flush=True)
            rng3 = np.random.default_rng(303)
            start3 = random_perturb_sequence(best_a, rng3, 0.2, 0.45)
            a_out, j_out = md_optimize(start3, t3, seed=303, beta_start=15.0, beta_end=3000.0, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
                best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
                best_score = float(j_out)
                print(f"Perturbed MD improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

            remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if remaining > 30:
                t3b = min(remaining * 0.5, 140.0)
                if t3b > 15:
                    base_for_pair = a_out if (np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out <= best_score) else best_a
                    a_out2, j_out2 = exact_pair_polish(base_for_pair, t3b, seed=304, verbose=False)
                    if np.isfinite(j_out2) and j_out2 < best_score:
                        best_a = norm_sum1(a_out2)
                        best_score = float(j_out2)
                        print(f"Perturbed pair improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 4: length exploration --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 180 and best_score > 1.0:
        base_n = best_a.size
        candidates = set()
        for delta in (1000, 1500, 2000, -1000, -1500):
            nn = base_n + delta
            if 1200 <= nn <= 9000 and nn != base_n:
                candidates.add(nn)
        candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: abs(x - base_n))

        for nn in candidates[:2]:
            remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if remaining < 120:
                break
            print(f"Phase 4: length exploration, n={nn}, remaining={remaining:.0f}s", flush=True)
            cand = interpolate_sequence(best_a, nn)

            t4a = min(remaining * 0.38, 130.0)
            if t4a > 15:
                a_out, j_out = exact_pair_polish(cand, t4a, seed=400 + nn % 97, verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
                    best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
                    best_score = float(j_out)
                    print(f"Length {nn} pair improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

            remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
            if remaining > 40:
                base_md = best_a if np.isfinite(best_score) else cand
                t4b = min(remaining * 0.38, 80.0)
                if t4b > 15:
                    a_out, j_out = md_optimize(base_md, t4b, seed=500 + nn % 97,
                                              beta_start=20.0, beta_end=2000.0, verbose=False)
                    if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
                        best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
                        best_score = float(j_out)
                        print(f"Length {nn} MD improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # -------- Phase 5: final exact polish --------
    remaining = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 15 and best_score > 1.0:
        t5 = min(remaining * 0.7, 140.0)
        if t5 > 10:
            print(f"Final polish: budget={t5:.0f}s", flush=True)
            a_out, j_out = exact_pair_polish(best_a, t5, seed=606, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(j_out) and j_out < best_score:
                best_a = norm_sum1(a_out)
                best_score = float(j_out)
                print(f"Final pair improved: {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Final normalization, sanitization, and validation.
    final_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    final_a = np.maximum(final_a, 0.0)
    final_a = final_a / final_a.sum()

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_a]
    final_eval = float("inf")
    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = score_normalized(final_a)

    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
        # Very safe fallback: moderately long Gaussian-ish positive sequence.
        n_fb = 4000
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_fb)
        fb = np.exp(-((x - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * 0.25 * 0.25))
        fb = norm_sum1(fb)
        final_list = [float(v) for v in fb]
        final_eval = float("inf")
        try:
            final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        except Exception:
            final_eval = score_normalized(fb)

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print(f"FINAL: score={final_eval:.6f}, n={len(final_list)}, time={elapsed:.0f}s", flush=True)
    return final_list
```
